Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The computing operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 191. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 867 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to computing theory, we decompose the problem into 191 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 867. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
